# Stroom GWT UI — file inventory & port categorisation

> Generated by `stroom-stroom-gwt-suite/tools/build-inventory.mjs` from the Stroom source.
> Scope: `src/main` files under a `client`/`shared`/`api` package segment, extensions
> `.java` / `.ui.xml` / `.gwt.xml` / `.css` (tests excluded). Files **not** compiled into
> the GWT app are excluded — a package is GWT-translatable only where a `.gwt.xml`
> `<source path>` whitelists it, so most of the server-side `*.api` layer is left out.
> Paths are relative to the
> `stroom` project root. Categorisation is heuristic (path + filename role); tune the rule
> inputs at the top of the build script and re-run. The companion `gwt-inventory.csv` is the
> machine-readable source of truth. Port status is overlaid from the durable
> `oracles/port-status.tsv` (edit that + re-run; regenerating never clobbers status).

**Total files: 3701**  ·  client 2652 · shared 966 · api 83

**Port status:** 25% of 3461 in-scope files · ported 0 · generated 860 · partial 0 · deferred 0 · to-do 2601 · out-of-scope 240

_Status legend: `ported` (`done` is a synonym, used on the Dashboard rows) · `generated` (OpenAPI codegen) · `partial` (core done) · `wip` (in progress) · `deferred` (port with its screen) · `todo` · `n/a` (out of scope — GWT framework plumbing React replaces wholesale: GIN DI, event bus, GWTP MVP, vendored GWT widget/cell overrides, the popup + tab event frameworks; excluded from the % denominator)._
